# What is Election (New Testament)

**[00:00:00]** Election is unfortunately a very controversial topic these days, yet the New Testament especially

**[00:00:10]** teaches it very plainly. Today we're going to see what the Bible has to say about this topic,

**[00:00:14]** and why election, which is the teaching that God chooses who is saved,

**[00:00:18]** is the best possible news we could have.

**[00:03:40]** They were made before time began and

**[00:03:43]** before history was ever a thing. God chose unconditionally, before all conditions,

**[00:04:08]** When he chose Abraham and David and Saul and Solomon and everybody, all the prophets, all the kings, all the messengers, Moses,
